# Graph Report - ungasis  (2026-06-02)

## Corpus Check
- 1499 files · ~1,240,892 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 19045 nodes · 17715 edges · 3073 communities (2033 shown, 1040 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 147 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `ad3e1b6f`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
